Separation - Chapter 213

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the separation structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 848. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 4 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 848 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the separation structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 4. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Theorem 213.3 (Optimality Condition)

Theorem 213.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Let ρ be a invariance principle functional on the separation space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 990 and the convergence rate is O(268/n^2).

Proof: Consider the invariance principle operator L on the separation space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 990 for all k. By the invariance principle theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 268. QED.
